# Research Log

Daily pre-market research entries are appended here in chronological
order. New entries go at the bottom.

---

## Format reference (do not delete)

```
## YYYY-MM-DD — Pre-market Research

### Account
- Equity: $X
- Cash: $X
- Buying power: $X
- Daytrade count: N

### Market Context
- WTI / Brent oil:
- S&P 500 futures:
- VIX:
- Today's catalysts:
- Earnings before open:
- Economic calendar (CPI / PPI / FOMC / jobs / etc.):
- Sector momentum (YTD or recent week):

### Trade Ideas
1. TICKER — catalyst (source), entry $X, stop $X, target $X, R:R X:1, sector
   - Direction: bullish | bearish
   - Materiality: 0.X (must be ≥ 0.6 to enter)
   - Horizon: N trading days (default 10)
   - Catalyst type: earnings | guidance | contract | regulatory | macro | sector_rotation
2. ...

### Risk Factors
- ...

### Decision
TRADE or HOLD (default HOLD if no clear edge)
```

## 2026-05-05 — Pre-market Research (after-hours, queued for 2026-05-06 open)

### Account
- Equity: $100,000 (sized as if $10,000 per CLAUDE.md baseline; satellite frame)
- Cash: $100,000
- Buying power: $200,000
- Daytrade count: 0

### Market Context
- WTI / Brent oil: not pulled (after-hours; revisit at pre-market tomorrow)
- S&P 500 futures: not pulled
- VIX: not pulled
- Today's catalysts: post-close earnings flood (QLYS, QRVO, COTY, SMCI, BL, BFAM, JOBY, LCID); Trump Truth Social post on Iran agreement / Strait of Hormuz pause (oil-tanker / SPY relevance)
- Earnings before open: not surveyed (revisit at pre-market tomorrow)
- Economic calendar: not surveyed
- Sector momentum: not surveyed; partial signal — cybersecurity SaaS strong on QLYS print

### Trade Ideas (3 of 3 weekly slots)

1. **QLYS** — Qualys raised FY2026 GAAP EPS guide ($5.20–5.48 → $5.40–5.61), raised Adj EPS guide ($7.17–7.45 → $7.44–7.65 vs $7.38 est), raised FY26 sales guide, Q2 sales guide $177.5–179.5M vs $177.1M est. Source: Benzinga earnings wire 2026-05-05 22:20–22:24 UTC. Multiple stacked positive data points. Sector: cybersecurity SaaS.
   - Entry: market open 2026-05-06; Stop: 10% trailing GTC; Target: +20% (R:R 2:1).
   - Falsification: by Fri close gives back >50% of post-earnings gap; or peer (CRWD/S/OKTA) sells off >5% on negative read-through; or any sector peer guides down within 2 weeks.

2. **QRVO** — Qorvo FY2027 Adj EPS guide $7.00 vs $6.60 est (~6% above forward-year). Source: Benzinga 2026-05-05 22:32 UTC. Sector: semiconductors (RF chips).
   - Entry: market open; Stop: 10% trailing GTC; Target: +20% (R:R 2:1).
   - Falsification: stock fades back below pre-earnings close within 5 trading days; or peer (SWKS, AVGO, QCOM) negative read-through within 2 weeks.

3. **COTY** — Coty FY2026 Adj EPS guide $0.33–0.35 vs $0.27 est (22–30% above). Source: Benzinga 2026-05-05 22:50 UTC. Sector: consumer beauty/personal care.
   - Entry: market open; Stop: 10% trailing GTC; Target: +20% (R:R 2:1).
   - Falsification: tomorrow's call commentary shows soft margins or FX-driven raise (not organic); or stock fails to sustain post-earnings move past day-2 close.
   - Note: stock is ~$2.55, microcap-adjacent, wide bid/ask post-close. Liquidity risk is real. Flagged for review.

### Risk Factors
- All three are post-earnings reactions. After-hours moves often partially reverse on the open as initial reaction is digested.
- Using all 3 weekly trade slots in one night — strategy says "patience > activity"; if any of the three look weak at open, skip rather than force.
- Iran/Hormuz Truth Social post is a wildcard for SPY and energy; could shift risk-on/risk-off sentiment.
- COTY liquidity (see note above).
- This is the first set of trades on the new evidence-based strategy frame; treat as calibration, not validation.

### Decision
TRADE — 3 market buys queued for 2026-05-06 open. Trailing stops to be placed once fills come back at /market-open tomorrow.

- **Sector momentum context:** Magnificent Seven megacaps still driving ~55% of Q-over-Q S&P 500 earnings growth. Cybersecurity SaaS (QLYS sector) showed strength on yesterday's prints; semis (QRVO sector) face read-through from AMD tonight.

Skipping — entire gainer/loser list is microcap junk (PHOE +168% at $46, SDOT +91% at $0.49, TALKW +70% at $0.0029). None pass liquidity / market-cap filter for our strategy.

No new news on QLYS, QRVO, or COTY since yesterday's after-hours earnings prints. Theses unchanged from yesterday's RESEARCH-LOG entry. Will revisit if AMD print tonight produces semi-sector read-through affecting QRVO.

**None today.** Reason: 3 of 3 weekly trade slots already used by yesterday's QLYS/QRVO/COTY queue. Strategy rule #8 (max 3 new trades/week) is binding.

3. Watch for AMD print tonight — may need to re-evaluate QRVO thesis tomorrow morning

This matches the falsification condition exactly: "soft margins or FX-driven raise (not organic)." The guide raise does not appear supported by organic fundamental improvement.
- **Per strategy rules: "If thesis has broken, close even if not yet at -7%."** This situation requires a sell decision.
- **Recommended action: SELL COTY at next available opportunity (market open today).** Do not wait for -7% hard stop. Thesis is broken.

*Weekly slots exhausted (3/3 used this week). These are watchlist entries for next week (week of 2026-05-11).*

Conditional: only if QLYS is closed and a slot opens, and only if sector momentum shifts. Lower priority.

### Risk Factors
- **COTY thesis broken** — immediate sell decision required at open. -5.5% unrealized, near falsification trigger. Do not let drift to -7%.
- **QRVO at -5.2%** — Skyworks merger overhang creates a hard ceiling. If stock resumes decline, cut at -7% ($86.90 approx). Only 1.8% of cushion remaining.

- **Iran deal fragility** — any reversal on Hormuz reopening would spike oil and risk-off broad market.
- **Slack notification failed** — keys not set. Cannot send alerts. All monitoring is manual this session.

### Decision
**HOLD QLYS. SELL COTY (thesis broken). WATCH QRVO (-5.2%, approaching hard stop).**

### Risk Factors

- **⭐⭐⭐ Iran-Israel full military exchange** — First direct missile exchange since April ceasefire. Oil +4–5%. Potential for Hormuz blockade. Wikipedia notes "economic impact of the 2026 Iran war." This is a REGIME-CHANGE event for the macro backdrop — not a passing headline. Risk-off could persist for weeks if escalation continues. Entire growth universe is repriced lower.
- **⭐⭐ NFP +172K (doubled forecast) → rate hike probability 72.7%** — The Fed narrative shifted Friday from "cuts later this year" to "hike possible." For a universe of high-P/E SaaS, AI, e-com names, this is severe multiple compression. NFP surprise + Iran escalation = dual macro headwinds. Worst backdrop since May 5 for new long entries.
- **AI/semi complex selling** — MU -11.5%, NVDA -7%, MRVL -13%. Even though AVGO's AI thesis is intact ($10.8B Q2, $16B Q3), the "is AI spending real?" question is back on the table. VRT is downstream of AI capex; if NVDA/AVGO keep selling, VRT will follow.
- **QRVO exit still pending (Day 33)** — The longest unresolved operational failure in this experiment's history. QRVO has held +9.6% gain. But it's in RF semis with active FTC/SAMR overhang and CEO insider selling. Every day of delay is pure binary risk.
- **QLYS analyst avg target below current price ($107.39 < $111.53)** — Not an exit trigger (trailing stop handles it), but worth noting. If the cybersecurity sector softens further (CRWD post-earnings overhang), QLYS could drift toward stop level.
- **API outage** — 33+ days. Cannot confirm positions, stops, or P&L. Cannot execute any trades regardless of setup quality. CRITICAL operational risk.
